Direction : Read the poem given below and 
answer the question that follow (Q. Nos. 91 to 96) by 
selecting the correct/most appropriate option. 
Gather ye rosebuds while ye may,  
Old Time is still a-flying; 
And this same flower that smiles today 
Tomorrow will be dying. 
The glorious lamp of heaven, the sun,  
The higher he's a-getting, 
The sooner will his race be run, 
And nearer he's to setting.  
That age is best which is the first,  
When youth and blood are warmer; 
But being spent, the worse, and worst 
Times still succeed the former. 
Then be not coy, but use your time, 
And while ye may, go marry;  
For having lost but once your prime, 
You may forever tarry. 
91.
The poem is addressed to a/an ______.
(a) Old friend
(b) Old unmarried woman
(c) Gardener
(d) Young woman
Ans. (d) : According to the sense of the above poem, 
the poem is addressed to a young woman to size the day 
and enjoy their youth.  
Hence, the correct answer is option (d).
92.

(b) Describe the glory of his light
(c) Describe the fast passage of time
(d) Show his warmth
Ans. (c) : The example of the sum is given to describe 
the fast passage of time. According o the 'Gather ye 
rosebuds while ye may, old time is still a - flying.  
Hence, the correct answer is option (c).
93.
What does the poet tell us about young age?
(a) It is a time to find one's love
(b) Time will son pass and it is a time to find
one's love
(c) It is a time to work hard
(d) Time will soon pass
Ans. (b) : According to sensed of the above poem the

it is a time to find one's love'.  
Hence, the correct answer is option (b).

Which of the following statements is true/false?

Gathering rosebuds is the same as enjoying

The sun can't keep on shining forever.

(a) Both A and B are true
(b) Both A and B are false
(c) A is true and B is false
(d) B is true and A is false

stanza of the poem, the above both statements 'A and B 
are true.
Other options are not correct. 
95.
'And this same flower that smiles today'.
Identify the figure of speed used in this line.
(a) Personification
(b) Hyperbole
(c) Simile
(d) Metaphor
Ans. (a) : The figure of speech of the above line 'And 
this 
same 
flower 
that 
smiles 
today' 
is 
used 
'Personification'. It is used to attribute human 
characteristics to something that is not human. It can 
also be used to personify an abstract quality.  
Hence, the correct answer is option (a).

comprised with each other without comparison word.

Directions: Read the passage given below and 
answer the questions that follow (Q. Nos. 97 to 105) 
by selecting the correct/most appropriate option.  
In the middle of her solo trip to Mizoram last month, 
Geeta Garud, 69, was walking on a narrow ledge, 
"making my way behind a waterfall, trying out 
something I wouldn't have in my younger years. And I 
realised how travelling solo was such a liberating 
experience." 
Before going on her week-long holiday, she was 
hesitant about travelling by herself. Garud had asked 
friends and family but found not takers. Yet there she 
was, alone, relishing a challenging trek. "Why was I 
being dependent on others? Being alone gives you a 
sense of freedom." 
She ended up having may firsts. She danced 
impromptu at a festival in Reiek, waded into a river, and 
went horse-riding. "I had opportunities to try horse-riding 
when I was younger, but could not work up the courage. 
This time, I thought, if not now, I may not get another 
chance, "says Garud, who was an athlete in her youth and 
had played cricket. 
She was at her farm in Koregaon during the 
lockdown and had felt the lack of social connections. It 
motivated her to travel as soon as things eased.  
"Travel is also about meeing people; I felt that sense 
of joy and freedom when I finally did it". She is planning 
to travel to Europe next and has decided not to take a 
friend or relative along. She intends to revisit the joy of 
travelling alone and relishing a sense of freedom all over 
again.     
97.
Which of the following statements is incorrect?
Travelling alone in distant Mizoram at the age
of 69 :
(a) She was breathing an air of freedom
(b) Was very enjoyable but very tiring
(c) Was a novel experience for Garud
(d) She was taking a big risk
Ans. (a) : According to the sense of the above passage, 
all the given statement true except option (a) 'she was 
breathing on air of freedom'.  
Hence, the correct option is (a).  
98.
Garud travelled alone as:
(a) She did not like company
(b) A companion would have offered her unasked
for advice.
(c) It was less expensive
(d) No one agreed to accompany her
Ans. (d) : According to the sense of the second passage 
garud travelled alone as: no one agreed to accompany 
her because Garud had asked friends and family but 
found not takers. Yet there she was, alone, relishing a 
challenging trek.  
Hence, the correct answer is option (d).

Choose the correct option :
(a) Both A and B are true
(b) Both A and B are false
(c) A is true and B is false
(d) B is true and A is false
Ans. (c) : According to the sense of the second passage, 
the above statements 'A' is true and 'B' is false, because 
initially, she did not like to go alone. She asked friends 
and family but found not takers.

102.
"And I realised  how travelling solo was ........ ." 
The underlined expression is a/an_____ clause.
(a) Adjective
(b) Adverb
(c) Principal
(d) Noun
Ans. (c) : the line of the above passage, the underlined 
word 'I realised' is a 'principal clause' and other part is a 
subordinate clause.  
Note- A principal clause is that part, which alone can 
make a complete sense, which is independent.  
Thus, the correct answer is the option (c).   
103.
'Garud had asked friends and family but found
no takers.'
The nuderlined word is a/an ______.
(a) Conjunction
(b) Interjection
(c) Noun
(d) Pronoun
Ans. (a) : The sentence of the above passage, the 
underlined word 'but' is a 'Conjunction'. It is a 
coordinating conjunction used to connect ideas that 
contrast.  
Hence, the correct answer is option (a).

106.
Read the following statements and choose the
correct option :
Assertion (A) : Use of phonetic spelling slows
down the ability to learn to spell correctly.
Reason (R) : When young children use spellings
that they 'invented' rather than conventional
spellings, it does not slow down their ability to
learn to spell correctly.
(a) (A) is true, but (R) is false
(b) (A) is false, but (R) is true
(c) Both (A) and (R) are true and (R) is the
correct explanation of (A)
(d) Both (A) and (R) are true, but (R) is the
correct explanation of (A)
Ans. (b) : For the above statements, the most 
appropriate option is (b) '(A) is false, but (R) is true'.  
Other options are not correct.
107.
In learning a new language, many students
often lack confidence when speaking due to the
language's unique pronunciation rules.
One way to overcome this problem is by:
(a) Conducting special speech therapy with a
counsellor and practice through drills
(b) Correcting errors whenever they happen
(c) Letting children read aloud in class
(d) Using game-like activities that require verbal
interaction in the classroom.
Ans. (d) : The most appropriate meaning of the above 
statements is option (d) 'Using game -like activates that 
require verbal interaction in the classroom. By its way 
to overcome this problem. 
Hence, the correct answer is option (d).
108.
A teacher of class III is presenting new
vocabulary to the learners. Which one of the
following is an effective way to present the new
vocabulary?
(a) The teacher should try to explain a word's
meaning in the situation in which it occurs.
(b) The teacher should write its meaning on the
blackboard and ask learners to learn it by
heart
(c) The teacher should tell them the antonyms of
words and give the meaning in their language
(d) The teacher should give them a concise
definition of words
Ans. (a) : An effective way to present the new 
vocabulary is option (a) 'The teacher should try to 
explain a word's meaning in the situation in which it 
occurs', when a teacher of class III is presenting new 
vocabulary to the learners.  
Other options are not correct.  
109.
Learning a language is a matter of _______.
(a) Product
(b) Skill
(c) Acquisition
(d) Process
Ans. (b) : Leaning a language is a matter of 'skill' be- 
cause language is the primary basis of all communication 
and the primary instrument of thought and skill. 
Learning a language is an art and not a silence as art and 
language both are used to express inner feelings and 
ideas. Thus, the correct option is (b).

111.
Choose the strategy that matches the example.
'I focus on the language I use to make sure it is
correct'.
(a) Self-monitoring
(b) Translanguaging
(c) Memorising
(d) Revising
Ans. (a) : The correct strategy of the example 'I focus 
on the language I use to make sure it is correct' is 'self -
monitoring'. It is a strategy that teaches students to self - 
assess their behaviors and record the results.  
Hence, the correct option is (a).
112.
Students is Class II sing a song, 'Head and
shoulders, knees and toes'. They touch the
correct part of their body as they sing the song.
Which method is the teacher using to teach
them?
(a) Total Physical Response
(b) Eclectic
(c) Audio-lingual
(d) Communicative Language Teaching
Ans. (a) : For the above statement the method 'Total 
physical Response (TPR)' is used to teach them by the 
teacher. It is a method of teaching language of 
vocabulary concepts by using physical movement to 
react to verbal input. It was created by American 
psychologist Dr. James Asher.  
Other options are not correct.
113.
Students work in groups to answer five
questions about vocabulary from the previous
unit. Then, they exchange questions with
another group and try to answer the question.
This is an example of:
(a) Reading assessment (b) Peer assessment
(c) Self-assessment
(d) Feedback
Ans. (b) : The above given statements is an example of 
'Peer assessment'. It is a process in which students 
provide feedback to other students. The purpose of this 
feedback is to help classmates improve their learning.  
Hence, the correct answer is option (b).

115.
Read the following statements and choose the
correct option :
Assertion (A) : All children who enter school
are competent language users according to
their age.
Reason (R) : Due to language and cultural
diversity, they are unable to demonstrate their
abilities.
(a) (A) is true, but (R) is false
(b) (A) is false, but (R) is true
(c) Both (A) and (R) are true and (R) is the
correct explanation of (A)
(d) Both (A) and (R) are true, but (R) is not the
correct explanation of (A)
Ans. (a) : 'All children who enter school are competent 
language users according to their age' this statement is 
correct whereas the statement that due to language and 
cultural diversity, they are unable to demonstrate their 
abilities is incorrect so, the correct option is (a) '(A) is 
true, but (R) is false'.  
Other option are not correct.
116.
Manjeet of Class V speaks in different ways to
his teacher, to his friends and also to a two-
year-old child. It means Mangeet knows how to
use language in social situations. What is this
attribute of a language known as?
(a) Pragmatics
(b) Act of speech
(c) Naturalist
(d) Semantics
Ans. (a) : For the above statements, the attribute of a 
language is known as 'Progmatics'. It focuses on asking 
learners to determine the best way to communicated in a 
certain situation given the context and the culture.  
Other option give different meanings. 
117.
A mother notices that her child sometimes says
things that she has never heard adults or her
siblings say. She is confused, as she believes
that the child learns language by imitating
people 
in 
her 
family 
and 
immediate
surroundings. Her belief resonates with:
(a) Constructivism
(b) Multilingualism
(c) Nativism
(d) Behaviourism
Ans. (d) : For the above mother description, her belief 
resonates with 'Behaviourism' by this we look at the 
observable actions of students and assesses whether 
they are learning as effectively as possible. 
Hence, the correct answer is option (d).
118.
The set of skills that develop before children
begin formal reading instruction and which
provide the foundation for later academic skill is :
(a) Emergent properties  (b) Emergent literacy
(c) Emergent solution 
(d) Emergent curriculum
Ans. (b) : The set of skills that develop before children 
begin formal reading instruction and which provide the 
foundation for later academic skill is known as 
'Emergent literacy'. 
119.
A grandmother loves to read out stories from
books to young children at home and, in this
way, exposes them to books and new ideas,
process. This technique is known as:
(a) Dialogic reading
(b) Aloud reading
(c) Model reading
(d) Shared reading
Ans. (a) : For the above statements, the technique 
which is used by mother, know as 'Dialogic reading'. It 
is the process of having a dialogue with students 
involves asking question to help children explore the 
text at a deeper level, including defining new being able 
to talk the text.
120.
An approach to teaching reading that starts
with basic elements like letters and phonemes
and teaches children that phonemes can be
combined into words before moving on to
reading as a whole is:
(a) Whole language approach
(b) Structural approach
(c) Top-down approach
(d) Bottom-up approach
Ans. (d) :  An approach to teaching reading that starts 
with basic elements like letters and phonemes and 
phonemes and teaches children that phonemes can be 
combined into words before moving on to reading as a 
whale is known as - 'bottom- up approach'.  
Thus, the correct answer is option (d).

Language-II : English/Deb«espeer 
Directons : Answer the following questions by 
selecting the correctmost appropriate option.
121.
A teacher can develop the listening skills of
language learners by :
(a) creating opportunities for learners to listen to
a variety of language sources and people and
engaging in other listening activities.
(b) making the learners listen to everything they
hear passively
(c) speaking continuously to learners both within
the classroom and outside
(d) focusing only on listening skills without
associating them with other language skills.
Ans. (a) : A teacher can develop the listening skills of 
language learners by creating opportunities for learners 
to listen to a variety of language sources and people and 
engaging in other listening activities.  
Hence, the correct answer is option (a).
122.
Read the following statements and choose the
correct option :
Assertion (A) :
Accuracy in language is most important at the
primary level.
yep
www.SarkariTeachers.com

===== PAGE 36 =====
CTET Class (1 to 5) 2023 (Exam date 20.08.2023) 
484 
YCT 
Reason (R) :  
Grammar is an integral part of the primary 
curriculum.
(a) (A) is true, but (R) is false.
(b) (A) is false, but (R) is true.
(c) Both (A) and (R) are true and (R) is the
correct explanation of (A).
(d) Both (A) and (R) are true, but (R) is not the
correct explanation of (A).
Ans. (b) : For the above statements, the correct option is 
(b) '(A) is false, but (R) is true'.
Accuracy in language is not most important at the
primary level but Grammar is an integral part of the
primary curriculum.
Other option are not correct.
123.
Theoretical positions and beliefs about the
nature of language, the nature of language
learning, and the applicability of both to
pedagogical settings is :
(a) Approach
(b) Technique
(c) Syllabus
(d) Content
Ans. (a) : Theoretical positions and beliefs about the 
nature of language, the nature of language learning, and 
the applicability of both to pedagogical setting is 
'approach'.  
Hence, the correct answer is option (a).
124.
Formative evaluation is :
(a) carried out at a pre-specified or particular
stage in the course.
(b) not subected to change.
(c) what is happening in the teaching-learning
process.
(d) related 
to
ongoing 
development 
and 
improvement.
Ans. (d) : Formative evaluation is related to ongoing 
development and improvement. It means an evaluation 
that provides an educator with information and 
assessments an how to improve the educator's 
performance.  
Hence, the correct option is (d).
125.
While planning lessons for young learners, the
teacher decided to focus on the Total Physical
Response (TPR) method. Which one of the
following should be used to include TPR in the
lesson?
(a) Must add some listening activity in class.
(b) Must keep activities aside and instead ask
questions and motivate them to respond.
(c) Must ensure to add some bodily activities in
class.
(d) Choose ativities that help them stay in their
seats and work in pairs or groups.
Ans. (c) : For the above description, the correct answer 
is option (c) 'Must ensure to add some bodily activities 
in class'.  
⇒Total physical Response (TPR) is a method of
teaching language or vocabulary concepts by using
physical movement to react to verbal input.
Hence, the correct answer is option (c).
126.
Which approach to grammar encourages the
belief that learning a language is a mater of
learning rules?
(a) Communicative
(b) Grammar Translation
(c) Deductive
(d) Inductive
Ans. (c) : 'Deductive' approach to grammar encourages 
the belief that learning a language is a mater of learning 
rules.  
Deductive method of teaching involves a teacher 
centered approach to presenting new content to students. 
This method is suitable for slow learners who need a 
clear base to start and are also more accustomed to the 
traditional approach.  
Hence, the correct answer is option (c).  
127.
A teacher is planning to give a free writing task
to learners in Class V. Which one of the
following should be focused on most by the
teacher?
(a) Both fluency and accuracy of the content
(b) The handwriting of the learner
(c) Accuracy of the content
(d) Fluency of the content
Ans. (d) : For the above statements, the correct option is 
(d) 'Fluency of the content' in which a teacher is
planning to given a free writing task to learners in class v.
Hence, the correct option is (d).
128.
Top-down means 'attend to the overall
meaning'. What does 'bottom-up' mean?
(a) Focus on the words and phrases of the text
(b) Focus on asking short answer type questions
(c) Focus on the gist of the text and abstract ideas
(d) Focus on pre-reading
Ans. (a) : According to the above statements 'Top-
down' means 'attend to the overall meaning' and 
'bottom-up' means ' Focus on the words and phrases of 
the text'.
Bottom-up learning is also known as implicit-to-explicit 
explication; and Top-down learning is also known as 
explicit-to-implicit implicitation.  
Thus, the correct answer is option (a).

129.
Which 
among 
these 
is 
the
primary 
responsibility of the language teacher?
(a) Grouping learners into different classes
(b) Instructing the learners
(c) Identifying the learner's communicative needs
(d) Selecting suitable content for teaching
Ans. (c) : 'Identifying the learner's communicative 
needs' is the primary responsibility of the language 
teacher.  
Other option are not correct.  
130.
A young child picks up a book, holds it right
side up, and turns the pages. These activities
demonstrate :
(a) emergent properties
(b) emergent solution skills
(c) emergent literacy skills
(d) emergent curriculum
Ans. (c) : 'When a young child picks up a book' holds it 
right side up, and turns the pages', skills'. These 
activities demons-trite 'emergent literal It is used to 
describe the reading and writing experience of young 
children before they learn to write and read 
conventionally.  
Thus, the correct answer is option (c).
131.
Read the following statements and choose the
correct option :
Assertion (A) :
Learners acquire languages since they are
genetically predisposed to do so and the
environment does not play any role in it.
Reason (R) :
The learner-centered classroom environment
has a great impact on language acquisition.
(a) (A) is true, but (R) is false.
(b) (A) is false, but (R) is true.
(c) Both (A) and (R) are true and (R) is the
correct explanation of (A).
(d) Both (A) and (R) are true, but (R) is not the
correct explanation of (A).
Ans. (b) : For the above statement, the correct answer, 
is option (b) '(A) is false, but (R) is true'.  
The correct statement of Assertion (A) Learners  
acquire languages since they are genetically pridisposed 
to do so and the environment also play a signification 
role in it as language development is a product of both 
heredity and environment.  
132.
A way to teach reading that emphasises
understanding the meaning of words from the
context in which they appear is:
(a) Whole language approach
(b) Structural approach
(c) Communicative approach
(d) Bottom-up approach
Ans. (a) : 'Whole language approach' is way to teach 
reading that emphasizes understanding the meaning of 
words from the context. It is an educational philosophy 
and instructional method for teaching language and 
literacy skills.
Hence, the correct option is (a).  
133.
The ability to think and talk about language is :
(a) Emergent literacy
(b) Metalinguistic ability
(c) Phonological awareness
(d) Proficiency
Ans. (b) : The ability to think and talk about language is 
known 'Metalinguistic ability'. It refers to an individual's 
ability to go beyond the literal meaning of language of 
manipulate language forms. Thus, the correct answer is 
option (b).
134.
A mother asked the teacher of Class II how it
was possible for her child to Sometimes say
things that she had never heard any adults or
siblings say. How is it possible?
Which one of the following is the apt answer to
this question?
(a) A child learns language when we reinforce
the development of the child's language.
(b) As per the constructivist approach, children
can invent new languages
(c) Children learn language only by imitating
adults.
(d) The human brain is innately wired to learn
language.
Ans. (d) : For the above statements, the correct answer 
is option (d) 'the human brain is innately wired to learn 
language'.  
Other option are not correct.
135.
A teachnique used to facilitate early literacy,
which involves an adult and a child looking at a
book together while the adult asks questions
and encourages a dialogue, followed by
switching roles so that the child asks questions
to the adult, is called:
(a) Dialogic reading
(b) Storytelling
(c) Model reading
(d) Shared reading
Ans. (a) : For the above statement, the correct option is 
(a) 'Dialogic reading'.
In dialogic reading, the adult helps the child become the 
teller of the story. The adult becomes the listener, the 
questioner, the audience for the child. No one can learn 
to read ust by listening to someone else read.  
Thus, the correct option is (a)

Directions : Read the passage given below and 
answer the questions that follow (Q. Nos. 136 to 143) 
by selecting the correctmost appropriate option. 
Dorothy lived in the midst of the great Kansas 
prairies (grasslands) with Uncle Henry, who was a 
farmer, and Aunt Em, who was the farmer's wife. Their 
house was small, for the lumber to build it bad to be 
carried by wagon from many miles afar. There were four 
walls, a floor and a roof, which made one room; and this 
room contained a rusty looking cooking stove, a 
cupboard for the dishes, a table, three or four chairs, and 
the beds. 
Uncle Henry and Aunt Em had a big bed in one 
corner, and Dorothy a little bed in another corner. There 
was no garret at all, and no cellar-except a small hole dug 
in the ground, called a cyclone cellar, where the family 
could go in case one of those great whirlwinds arose, 
mighty enough to crush any building in its path. It was 
reached by a trapdoor in the middle of the floor, from 
which a ladder led down into the small, dark hole.  
When Dorothy stood in the doorway and looked 
around, she could see nothing but the great gray prairie 
on every side. Not a tree nor a house broke the broad 
sweep of flat country that reached to the edge of the sky 
in all directions. The sun had baked the ploughed land 
into a gray mass, with little cracks running through it. 
Even the grass was not green, for the sun had burned the 
tops of long blades until they were the same gray colour 
to be seen everywhere. Once the house had been painted, 
but the sun blistered the paint and the rains washed it 
away, and now the house was as dull and gray as 
everything else.

(b) Uncle Henry's house was freshly painted.
(c) Dorothy's parents were rich farmers
(d) Uncle Henry had a big farm.
Ans. (a) : According to the sense of the above first 
passage, the correct option is (a) 'She lived in a 
smalland shabby house'.  
'Shabby' means - 'Looking old and in bad condition 
because of being used for a long time.

available.

the house'. because Dorothy lived in the midest of the 
great Kansas prairies (Grasslands) with Unnel Henry's 
in small house.  
Other option are correct.

Directions : Read the passage given below and 
answer the questions that follow (Q. Nos. 144 to 150) 
by selecting the correctmost appropriate option.  
Father would dress himself for court in a brightly 
coloured dhoti, a matching white shirt with an equally 
bright white turban and a neat black coat. The 
paraphernalia to court would include a cloth bundle 
containing court papers and a basket containing hot 
coffee with tumblers and plates for tiffin given by my 
mother or sisters-in-law. 
The younger advocates of his time were great 
admirers of my father and used to listen to him with rapt 
attention whenever he rose to address the court. His 
arguments were forceful, coherent, cogent, compulsive 
and conclusive. The arguments would go on till about 
lunchtime and even the English udge used to take down 
notes of his points.  
One client was particular that my father alone should 
argue his case. On the day my mother died, this case 
happened to be on the cause list. My mother was alive 
when my father left for court at 10 a.m. that morning and 
he had told my brother that he would return as soon as 
the case was over. Unfortunately, she died within an hour 
of his departure.  
With some difficulty, the news was conveyed 
through an advocate in court. My father, however, 
continued his arguments without showing any emotions. 
Only after concluding the case did he get back home to 
the room where my mother's body lay and stood in stoic 
silence.

Hence, the correct option is (c).
